• Added appendix/toolkit/library/bytebytego-resource/librarian/scripts/bytebytego_librarian.py (+713 -0)
      1 +#!/usr/bin/env python3
      2 +"""ByteByteGo Librarian runner.
      3 +
      4 +Implements:
      5 +question -> TOC selection -> folder scan -> notes -> synthesis
      6 +with ticketing + check-out/check-in history.
      7 +"""
      8 +
      9 +from __future__ import annotations
     10 +
     11 +import argparse
     12 +import csv
     13 +import json
     14 +import re
     15 +import sys
     16 +from collections import Counter
     17 +from dataclasses import dataclass
     18 +from datetime import datetime
     19 +from pathlib import Path
     20 +from typing import Iterable, List, Optional, Sequence
     21 +
     22 +
     23 +STATUS_VALUES = {"OPEN", "IN_PROGRESS", "RESOLVED", "BLOCKED"}
     24 +
     25 +STOP_WORDS = {
     26 +    "the",
     27 +    "and",
     28 +    "for",
     29 +    "with",
     30 +    "that",
     31 +    "this",
     32 +    "from",
     33 +    "into",
     34 +    "what",
     35 +    "when",
     36 +    "where",
     37 +    "which",
     38 +    "while",
     39 +    "your",
     40 +    "about",
     41 +    "have",
     42 +    "will",
     43 +    "just",
     44 +    "into",
     45 +    "how",
     46 +    "can",
     47 +    "you",
     48 +    "are",
     49 +    "our",
     50 +    "out",
     51 +    "all",
     52 +    "use",
     53 +}
     54 +
     55 +TOC_RE = re.compile(r"^(.*?)\s*\.{4}\s*(\d+)$")
     56 +PAGE_FILE_RE = re.compile(r"_Page_(\d{3})\.png$")
     57 +
     58 +
     59 +@dataclass
     60 +class TocEntry:
     61 +    idx: int
     62 +    section_id: str
     63 +    title: str
     64 +    start_page: int
     65 +    end_page: int
     66 +    folder: Optional[Path]
     67 +    score: int = 0
     68 +
     69 +
     70 +def resource_root() -> Path:
     71 +    # .../bytebytego-resource/librarian/scripts/bytebytego_librarian.py
     72 +    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
     73 +
     74 +
     75 +def librarian_root() -> Path:
     76 +    return resource_root() / "librarian"
     77 +
     78 +
     79 +def toc_file() -> Path:
     80 +    return resource_root() / "2-table-of-contents" / "2-table-of-contents_ocr.txt"
     81 +
     82 +
     83 +def learnings_root() -> Path:
     84 +    return resource_root() / "3-learnings"
     85 +
     86 +
     87 +def tickets_dir() -> Path:
     88 +    return librarian_root() / "tickets"
     89 +
     90 +
     91 +def history_log() -> Path:
     92 +    return librarian_root() / "history" / "checkout_log.csv"
     93 +
     94 +
     95 +def sessions_runs_dir() -> Path:
     96 +    return librarian_root() / "sessions" / "runs"
     97 +
     98 +
     99 +def now_iso() -> str:
    100 +    return datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    101 +
    102 +
    103 +def now_stamp() -> str:
    104 +    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    105 +
    106 +
    107 +def ensure_workspace() -> None:
    108 +    (librarian_root() / "history").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    109 +    (librarian_root() / "tickets").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    110 +    (librarian_root() / "sessions" / "runs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    111 +    log_path = history_log()
    112 +    if not log_path.exists():
    113 +        log_path.write_text(
    114 +            "timestamp,event,ticket_id,status,actor,question,section_ids,session_dir,notes\n",
    115 +            encoding="utf-8",
    116 +        )
    117 +
    118 +
    119 +def parse_page_num(path: Path) -> Optional[int]:
    120 +    match = PAGE_FILE_RE.search(path.name)
    121 +    if not match:
    122 +        return None
    123 +    return int(match.group(1))
    124 +
    125 +
    126 +def detect_max_page() -> int:
    127 +    root = learnings_root()
    128 +    pages = []
    129 +    for image in root.glob("bytebytego-resource_Page_*.png"):
    130 +        page = parse_page_num(image)
    131 +        if page is not None:
    132 +            pages.append(page)
    133 +    if not pages:
    134 +        for image in root.glob("**/bytebytego-resource_Page_*.png"):
    135 +            page = parse_page_num(image)
    136 +            if page is not None:
    137 +                pages.append(page)
    138 +    if not pages:
    139 +        raise RuntimeError("No learning images found for page-range resolution.")
    140 +    return max(pages)
    141 +
    142 +
    143 +def find_section_folder(section_id: str) -> Optional[Path]:
    144 +    root = learnings_root()
    145 +    candidates = []
    146 +    prefix = f"{section_id} "
    147 +    for item in sorted(root.iterdir()):
    148 +        if item.is_dir() and item.name.startswith(prefix):
    149 +            candidates.append(item)
    150 +    return candidates[0] if candidates else None
    151 +
    152 +
    153 +def load_toc_entries() -> List[TocEntry]:
    154 +    toc_path = toc_file()
    155 +    if not toc_path.exists():
    156 +        raise RuntimeError(f"TOC file not found: {toc_path}")
    157 +
    158 +    raw: List[tuple[str, int]] = []
    159 +    for line in toc_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
    160 +        line = line.strip()
    161 +        if not line:
    162 +            continue
    163 +        match = TOC_RE.match(line)
    164 +        if not match:
    165 +            continue
    166 +        title = match.group(1).strip()
    167 +        start_page = int(match.group(2))
    168 +        raw.append((title, start_page))
    169 +
    170 +    if not raw:
    171 +        raise RuntimeError(f"No TOC entries parsed from {toc_path}")
    172 +
    173 +    max_page = detect_max_page()
    174 +    entries: List[TocEntry] = []
    175 +    for idx, (title, start_page) in enumerate(raw, start=1):
    176 +        section_id = f"{idx:03d}"
    177 +        if idx < len(raw):
    178 +            end_page = raw[idx][1] - 1
    179 +        else:
    180 +            end_page = max_page
    181 +        folder = find_section_folder(section_id)
    182 +        entries.append(
    183 +            TocEntry(
    184 +                idx=idx,
    185 +                section_id=section_id,
    186 +                title=title,
    187 +                start_page=start_page,
    188 +                end_page=end_page,
    189 +                folder=folder,
    190 +            )
    191 +        )
    192 +    return entries
    193 +
    194 +
    195 +def tokenize(text: str) -> List[str]:
    196 +    words = re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", text.lower())
    197 +    return [w for w in words if len(w) >= 3 and w not in STOP_WORDS]
    198 +
    199 +
    200 +def score_entries(question: str, entries: Sequence[TocEntry]) -> List[TocEntry]:
    201 +    q_tokens = tokenize(question)
    202 +    q_set = set(q_tokens)
    203 +    q_bigrams = set()
    204 +    for i in range(0, max(len(q_tokens) - 1, 0)):
    205 +        q_bigrams.add(f"{q_tokens[i]} {q_tokens[i + 1]}")
    206 +
    207 +    scored: List[TocEntry] = []
    208 +    for entry in entries:
    209 +        title_tokens = tokenize(entry.title)
    210 +        common = q_set.intersection(title_tokens)
    211 +        score = len(common) * 3
    212 +
    213 +        title_lower = entry.title.lower()
    214 +        if question.strip().lower() and question.strip().lower() in title_lower:
    215 +            score += 8
    216 +        for bigram in q_bigrams:
    217 +            if bigram in title_lower:
    218 +                score += 2
    219 +        scored.append(
    220 +            TocEntry(
    221 +                idx=entry.idx,
    222 +                section_id=entry.section_id,
    223 +                title=entry.title,
    224 +                start_page=entry.start_page,
    225 +                end_page=entry.end_page,
    226 +                folder=entry.folder,
    227 +                score=score,
    228 +            )
    229 +        )
    230 +
    231 +    scored.sort(key=lambda e: (-e.score, e.idx))
    232 +    return scored
    233 +
    234 +
    235 +def pick_entries(entries: Sequence[TocEntry], section_ids: Optional[str], top_k: int, question: str) -> tuple[List[TocEntry], List[TocEntry]]:
    236 +    scored = score_entries(question, entries)
    237 +    if section_ids:
    238 +        wanted = {sid.strip() for sid in section_ids.split(",") if sid.strip()}
    239 +        selected = [e for e in entries if e.section_id in wanted]
    240 +        missing = sorted(wanted.difference({e.section_id for e in selected}))
    241 +        if missing:
    242 +            raise RuntimeError(f"Unknown section IDs: {', '.join(missing)}")
    243 +        return selected, scored
    244 +
    245 +    positives = [e for e in scored if e.score > 0]
    246 +    if positives:
    247 +        selected = positives[:top_k]
    248 +    else:
    249 +        selected = list(entries[:top_k])
    250 +    selected.sort(key=lambda e: e.idx)
    251 +    return selected, scored
    252 +
    253 +
    254 +def next_ticket_id() -> str:
    255 +    max_id = 1
    256 +    for ticket in tickets_dir().glob("LIB-*.yaml"):
    257 +        match = re.match(r"LIB-(\d{3})", ticket.stem)
    258 +        if not match:
    259 +            continue
    260 +        max_id = max(max_id, int(match.group(1)))
    261 +    return f"LIB-{max_id + 1:03d}"
    262 +
    263 +
    264 +def ticket_path_for(ticket_id: str) -> Path:
    265 +    return tickets_dir() / f"{ticket_id}.yaml"
    266 +
    267 +
    268 +def write_ticket(ticket_id: str, question: str, selected: Sequence[TocEntry], top_k: int, actor: str) -> Path:
    269 +    path = ticket_path_for(ticket_id)
    270 +    if path.exists():
    271 +        return path
    272 +
    273 +    title = question.strip().replace("\n", " ")
    274 +    if len(title) > 120:
    275 +        title = title[:120].rstrip() + "..."
    276 +
    277 +    section_ids = [e.section_id for e in selected]
    278 +    section_ranges = [f"{e.section_id}: {e.start_page:03d}-{e.end_page:03d}" for e in selected]
    279 +    content = [
    280 +        f'ticket_id: "{ticket_id}"',
    281 +        f'date: "{datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")}"',
    282 +        'status: "OPEN"',
    283 +        'ticket_type: "RESEARCH"',
    284 +        f'title: "{title}"',
    285 +        "",
    286 +        "research_question: |",
    287 +        f"  {question.strip()}",
    288 +        "",
    289 +        "toc_scope:",
    290 +        f'  toc_file: "{toc_file()}"',
    291 +        '  selection_mode: "auto"',
    292 +        f"  requested_top_k: {top_k}",
    293 +        "  selected_section_ids:",
    294 +    ]
    295 +    for sid in section_ids:
    296 +        content.append(f'    - "{sid}"')
    297 +    content.extend(
    298 +        [
    299 +            "",
    300 +            "selected_page_ranges:",
    301 +        ]
    302 +    )
    303 +    for section_range in section_ranges:
    304 +        content.append(f'  - "{section_range}"')
    305 +    content.extend(
    306 +        [
    307 +            "",
    308 +            "folder_scan:",
    309 +            f'  source_root: "{learnings_root()}"',
    310 +            '  scan_mode: "ocr"',
    311 +            "",
    312 +            "deliverables:",
    313 +            "  require_selection_map: true",
    314 +            "  require_folder_notes: true",
    315 +            "  require_synthesis_answer: true",
    316 +            "  require_checkout_and_checkin_log: true",
    317 +            "",
    318 +            "meta:",
    319 +            f'  actor: "{actor}"',
    320 +        ]
    321 +    )
    322 +    path.write_text("\n".join(content) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    323 +    return path
    324 +
    325 +
    326 +def update_ticket_status(ticket_id: str, status: str) -> None:
    327 +    status = status.upper()
    328 +    if status not in STATUS_VALUES:
    329 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid status: {status}")
    330 +    path = ticket_path_for(ticket_id)
    331 +    if not path.exists():
    332 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Ticket not found: {path}")
    333 +    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    334 +    if re.search(r'^status:\s*".*"$', text, flags=re.MULTILINE):
    335 +        text = re.sub(r'^status:\s*".*"$', f'status: "{status}"', text, count=1, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    336 +    else:
    337 +        text += f'\nstatus: "{status}"\n'
    338 +    path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    339 +
    340 +
    341 +def append_history(
    342 +    event: str,
    343 +    ticket_id: str,
    344 +    status: str,
    345 +    actor: str,
    346 +    question: str,
    347 +    section_ids: Sequence[str],
    348 +    session_dir: Path,
    349 +    notes: str,
    350 +) -> None:
    351 +    ensure_workspace()
    352 +    with history_log().open("a", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
    353 +        writer = csv.writer(handle)
    354 +        writer.writerow(
    355 +            [

    357 +                event,
    358 +                ticket_id,
    359 +                status,
    360 +                actor,
    361 +                question.replace("\n", " ").strip(),
    362 +                ",".join(section_ids),
    363 +                str(session_dir),
    364 +                notes.replace("\n", " ").strip(),
    365 +            ]
    366 +        )
    367 +
    368 +
    369 +def import_ocr_deps():
    370 +    try:
    371 +        import easyocr  # type: ignore
    372 +        import numpy as np  # type: ignore
    373 +        from PIL import Image  # type: ignore
    374 +    except Exception as exc:
    375 +        raise RuntimeError(
    376 +            "OCR dependencies are missing. Install `easyocr`, `numpy`, `Pillow`."
    377 +        ) from exc
    378 +    return easyocr, np, Image
    379 +
    380 +
    381 +def image_files_for_entry(entry: TocEntry, max_images: Optional[int]) -> List[Path]:
    382 +    if entry.folder is None or not entry.folder.exists():
    383 +        return []
    384 +    images = sorted(
    385 +        entry.folder.glob("bytebytego-resource_Page_*.png"),
    386 +        key=lambda p: parse_page_num(p) or 0,
    387 +    )
    388 +    if max_images is not None:
    389 +        images = images[: max(0, max_images)]
    390 +    return images
    391 +
    392 +
    393 +def ocr_images(reader, np, Image, images: Sequence[Path]) -> List[tuple[Path, List[str]]]:
    394 +    out: List[tuple[Path, List[str]]] = []
    395 +    for image in images:
    396 +        arr = np.array(Image.open(image).convert("RGB"))
    397 +        lines = reader.readtext(arr, detail=0, paragraph=False)
    398 +        cleaned = []
    399 +        for line in lines:
    400 +            text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", str(line)).strip()
    401 +            if text:
    402 +                cleaned.append(text)
    403 +        out.append((image, cleaned))
    404 +    return out
    405 +
    406 +
    407 +def dedupe_keep_order(lines: Iterable[str]) -> List[str]:
    408 +    seen = set()
    409 +    out = []
    410 +    for line in lines:
    411 +        key = line.lower().strip()
    412 +        if not key or key in seen:
    413 +            continue
    414 +        seen.add(key)
    415 +        out.append(line.strip())
    416 +    return out
    417 +
    418 +
    419 +def build_section_note(question: str, entry: TocEntry, ocr_dump: Sequence[tuple[Path, List[str]]]) -> tuple[str, List[str]]:
    420 +    all_lines: List[str] = []
    421 +    for _, lines in ocr_dump:
    422 +        all_lines.extend(lines)
    423 +
    424 +    all_lines = dedupe_keep_order(all_lines)
    425 +    all_lines = [line for line in all_lines if not re.fullmatch(r"\d+", line)]
    426 +    highlights = all_lines[:12]
    427 +
    428 +    q_tokens = set(tokenize(question))
    429 +    content_tokens = [tok for tok in tokenize(" ".join(all_lines)) if tok not in STOP_WORDS]
    430 +    top_terms = [term for term, _ in Counter(content_tokens).most_common(10)]
    431 +    overlap = sorted(q_tokens.intersection(top_terms))
    432 +
    433 +    lines = []
    434 +    lines.append(f"# Section {entry.section_id}: {entry.title}")
    435 +    lines.append("")
    436 +    lines.append(f"- pages: `{entry.start_page:03d}-{entry.end_page:03d}`")
    437 +    lines.append(f"- folder: `{entry.folder}`")
    438 +    lines.append(f"- images scanned: `{len(ocr_dump)}`")
    439 +    lines.append(f"- question overlap terms: `{', '.join(overlap) if overlap else 'none'}`")
    440 +    lines.append("")
    441 +    lines.append("## Key Notes")
    442 +    if highlights:
    443 +        for item in highlights:
    444 +            lines.append(f"- {item}")
    445 +    else:
    446 +        lines.append("- No OCR text extracted.")
    447 +    lines.append("")
    448 +    lines.append("## Frequent Terms")
    449 +    if top_terms:
    450 +        lines.append("- " + ", ".join(top_terms))
    451 +    else:
    452 +        lines.append("- none")
    453 +    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n", highlights
    454 +
    455 +
    456 +def write_selection_md(path: Path, question: str, selected: Sequence[TocEntry], ranked: Sequence[TocEntry]) -> None:
    457 +    lines = []
    458 +    lines.append("# TOC Selection")
    459 +    lines.append("")
    460 +    lines.append(f"- question: {question}")
    461 +    lines.append("")
    462 +    lines.append("## Selected Sections")
    463 +    for entry in selected:
    464 +        lines.append(
    465 +            f"- [{entry.section_id}] {entry.title} | pages {entry.start_page:03d}-{entry.end_page:03d} | score={entry.score}"
    466 +        )
    467 +        lines.append(f"  folder: `{entry.folder}`")
    468 +    lines.append("")
    469 +    lines.append("## Top Ranked (Preview)")
    470 +    for entry in ranked[:12]:
    471 +        lines.append(f"- [{entry.section_id}] score={entry.score} {entry.title}")
    472 +    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    473 +
    474 +
    475 +def write_synthesis(path: Path, question: str, selected: Sequence[TocEntry], highlights_by_section: dict[str, List[str]]) -> None:
    476 +    lines = []
    477 +    lines.append("# Synthesized Response")
    478 +    lines.append("")
    479 +    lines.append(f"## Question")
    480 +    lines.append(question)
    481 +    lines.append("")
    482 +    lines.append("## Evidence by Section")
    483 +    for entry in selected:
    484 +        lines.append(f"- [{entry.section_id}] {entry.title} (pages {entry.start_page:03d}-{entry.end_page:03d})")
    485 +        for item in highlights_by_section.get(entry.section_id, [])[:4]:
    486 +            lines.append(f"  - {item}")
    487 +    lines.append("")
    488 +    lines.append("## Response Draft")
    489 +    lines.append("Based on the selected ByteByteGo sections:")
    490 +    for entry in selected:
    491 +        section_highlights = highlights_by_section.get(entry.section_id, [])
    492 +        if section_highlights:
    493 +            lines.append(f"- [{entry.section_id}] {entry.title}: {section_highlights[0]}")
    494 +        else:
    495 +            lines.append(f"- [{entry.section_id}] {entry.title}: no extractable text found in scanned images.")
    496 +    lines.append("")
    497 +    lines.append("## Citation Index")
    498 +    for entry in selected:
    499 +        lines.append(f"- [{entry.section_id}] `{entry.folder}`")
    500 +    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    501 +
    502 +
    503 +def run_research(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    504 +    ensure_workspace()
    505 +    entries = load_toc_entries()
    506 +    selected, ranked = pick_entries(entries, args.section_ids, args.top_k, args.question)
    507 +    if not selected:
    508 +        raise RuntimeError("No sections selected.")
    509 +
    510 +    ticket_id = args.ticket_id or next_ticket_id()
    511 +    ticket_path = write_ticket(ticket_id, args.question, selected, args.top_k, args.actor)
    512 +    update_ticket_status(ticket_id, "IN_PROGRESS")
    513 +
    514 +    run_id = now_stamp()
    515 +    session_dir = sessions_runs_dir() / f"{run_id}__{ticket_id}"
    516 +    ocr_dir = session_dir / "ocr"
    517 +    notes_dir = session_dir / "section_notes"
    518 +    session_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    519 +    ocr_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    520 +    notes_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    521 +
    522 +    write_selection_md(session_dir / "selection.md", args.question, selected, ranked)
    523 +
    524 +    section_ids = [entry.section_id for entry in selected]
    525 +    append_history(
    526 +        event="CHECKOUT",
    527 +        ticket_id=ticket_id,
    528 +        status="IN_PROGRESS",
    529 +        actor=args.actor,
    530 +        question=args.question,
    531 +        section_ids=section_ids,
    532 +        session_dir=session_dir,
    533 +        notes=args.notes or "",
    534 +    )
    535 +
    536 +    highlights_by_section: dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    537 +    scan_errors: List[str] = []
    538 +    scanned_images = 0
    539 +
    540 +    if not args.skip_scan:
    541 +        easyocr, np, Image = import_ocr_deps()
    542 +        reader = easyocr.Reader(["en"], gpu=False, verbose=False)
    543 +
    544 +        for entry in selected:
    545 +            images = image_files_for_entry(entry, args.max_images_per_section)
    546 +            if not images:
    547 +                scan_errors.append(f"{entry.section_id}: no images in folder {entry.folder}")
    548 +                note_text, highlights = build_section_note(args.question, entry, [])
    549 +                (notes_dir / f"{entry.section_id}.md").write_text(note_text, encoding="utf-8")
    550 +                highlights_by_section[entry.section_id] = highlights
    551 +                continue
    552 +
    553 +            dump = ocr_images(reader, np, Image, images)
    554 +            scanned_images += len(dump)
    555 +            for image, lines in dump:
    556 +                page_num = parse_page_num(image)
    557 +                page_part = f"{page_num:03d}" if page_num is not None else "unknown"
    558 +                (ocr_dir / f"{entry.section_id}_page_{page_part}.txt").write_text(
    559 +                    "\n".join(lines) + ("\n" if lines else ""),
    560 +                    encoding="utf-8",
    561 +                )
    562 +
    563 +            note_text, highlights = build_section_note(args.question, entry, dump)
    564 +            (notes_dir / f"{entry.section_id}.md").write_text(note_text, encoding="utf-8")
    565 +            highlights_by_section[entry.section_id] = highlights
    566 +    else:
    567 +        for entry in selected:
    568 +            note_text = (
    569 +                f"# Section {entry.section_id}: {entry.title}\n\n"
    570 +                "- scan skipped: true\n"
    571 +                "- guidance: rerun without `--skip-scan` to extract OCR evidence.\n"
    572 +            )
    573 +            (notes_dir / f"{entry.section_id}.md").write_text(note_text, encoding="utf-8")
    574 +            highlights_by_section[entry.section_id] = []
    575 +
    576 +    write_synthesis(session_dir / "synthesis.md", args.question, selected, highlights_by_section)
    577 +
    578 +    status = "RESOLVED"
    579 +    if args.skip_scan:
    580 +        status = "IN_PROGRESS"
    581 +    elif scan_errors:
    582 +        status = "BLOCKED"
    583 +    update_ticket_status(ticket_id, status)
    584 +
    585 +    manifest = {
    586 +        "run_id": run_id,
    587 +        "ticket_id": ticket_id,
    588 +        "ticket_path": str(ticket_path),
    589 +        "question": args.question,
    590 +        "selected_section_ids": section_ids,
    591 +        "selected_count": len(selected),
    592 +        "scan_skipped": bool(args.skip_scan),
    593 +        "scanned_images": scanned_images,
    594 +        "scan_errors": scan_errors,
    595 +        "status": status,
    596 +        "timestamp": now_iso(),
    597 +    }
    598 +    (session_dir / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    599 +
    600 +    if args.auto_checkin:
    601 +        append_history(
    602 +            event="CHECKIN",
    603 +            ticket_id=ticket_id,
    604 +            status=status,
    605 +            actor=args.actor,
    606 +            question=args.question,
    607 +            section_ids=section_ids,
    608 +            session_dir=session_dir,
    609 +            notes=args.notes or "",
    610 +        )
    611 +
    612 +    print(f"ticket_id={ticket_id}")
    613 +    print(f"ticket_path={ticket_path}")
    614 +    print(f"session_dir={session_dir}")
    615 +    print(f"status={status}")
    616 +    print(f"selected_sections={','.join(section_ids)}")
    617 +    print(f"scanned_images={scanned_images}")
    618 +    if scan_errors:
    619 +        print("scan_errors:")
    620 +        for err in scan_errors:
    621 +            print(f"- {err}")
    622 +    return 0
    623 +
    624 +
    625 +def run_checkin(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    626 +    ensure_workspace()
    627 +    ticket_id = args.ticket_id
    628 +    status = args.status.upper()
    629 +    if status not in STATUS_VALUES:
    630 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid status: {status}")
    631 +    update_ticket_status(ticket_id, status)
    632 +    append_history(
    633 +        event="CHECKIN",
    634 +        ticket_id=ticket_id,
    635 +        status=status,
    636 +        actor=args.actor,
    637 +        question=args.question or "",
    638 +        section_ids=[sid.strip() for sid in (args.section_ids or "").split(",") if sid.strip()],
    639 +        session_dir=Path(args.session_dir) if args.session_dir else sessions_runs_dir(),
    640 +        notes=args.notes or "",
    641 +    )
    642 +    print(f"ticket_id={ticket_id}")
    643 +    print(f"status={status}")
    644 +    return 0
    645 +
    646 +
    647 +def run_list_sections(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    648 +    entries = load_toc_entries()
    649 +    query_tokens = set(tokenize(args.query or ""))
    650 +    print("section_id | pages      | folder | title")
    651 +    print("-" * 120)
    652 +    for entry in entries:
    653 +        if query_tokens:
    654 +            title_tokens = set(tokenize(entry.title))
    655 +            if not query_tokens.intersection(title_tokens):
    656 +                continue
    657 +        folder_flag = "yes" if entry.folder else "no"
    658 +        print(
    659 +            f"{entry.section_id} | {entry.start_page:03d}-{entry.end_page:03d} | {folder_flag:>3} | {entry.title}"
    660 +        )
    661 +    return 0
    662 +
    663 +
    664 +def parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    665 +    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="ByteByteGo Librarian runner")
    666 +    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    667 +
    668 +    research = sub.add_parser("research", help="Run question -> selection -> scan -> notes -> synthesis")
    669 +    research.add_argument("--question", required=True, help="Research question")
    670 +    research.add_argument("--ticket-id", help="Reuse an existing ticket ID (e.g., LIB-002)")
    671 +    research.add_argument("--top-k", type=int, default=5, help="Number of sections to select when auto-routing")
    672 +    research.add_argument("--section-ids", help="Comma-separated explicit section IDs, e.g. 001,070,149")
    673 +    research.add_argument("--skip-scan", action="store_true", help="Skip OCR scanning and generate planning artifacts only")
    674 +    research.add_argument("--max-images-per-section", type=int, help="Optional cap for OCR images per selected section")
    675 +    research.add_argument("--actor", default="codex", help="Actor name for history log")
    676 +    research.add_argument("--notes", help="Free-form notes for history log")
    677 +    research.add_argument(
    678 +        "--no-auto-checkin",
    679 +        action="store_false",
    680 +        dest="auto_checkin",
    681 +        help="Do not emit automatic CHECKIN event",
    682 +    )
    683 +    research.set_defaults(auto_checkin=True, fn=run_research)
    684 +
    685 +    checkin = sub.add_parser("checkin", help="Manual check-in/update for a ticket")
    686 +    checkin.add_argument("--ticket-id", required=True)
    687 +    checkin.add_argument("--status", default="RESOLVED", help="OPEN | IN_PROGRESS | RESOLVED | BLOCKED")
    688 +    checkin.add_argument("--actor", default="codex")
    689 +    checkin.add_argument("--question", help="Optional question text for log")
    690 +    checkin.add_argument("--section-ids", help="Optional comma-separated section IDs for log")
    691 +    checkin.add_argument("--session-dir", help="Optional session path for log")
    692 +    checkin.add_argument("--notes", help="Check-in notes")
    693 +    checkin.set_defaults(fn=run_checkin)
    694 +
    695 +    list_sections = sub.add_parser("list-sections", help="List TOC sections with page ranges")
    696 +    list_sections.add_argument("--query", help="Optional keyword filter")
    697 +    list_sections.set_defaults(fn=run_list_sections)
    698 +
    699 +    return p
    700 +
    701 +
    702 +def main() -> int:
    703 +    args = parser().parse_args()
    704 +    try:
    705 +        return args.fn(args)
    706 +    except Exception as exc:
    707 +        print(f"Error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
    708 +        return 1
    709 +
    710 +
    711 +if __name__ == "__main__":
    712 +    raise SystemExit(main())
    713 +

  out/check-in history.

  What I added

  - System contracts:
      - appendix/toolkit/library/bytebytego-resource/librarian/system/CONSTITUTION.md
      - appendix/toolkit/library/bytebytego-resource/librarian/system/METHODOLOGY.md

  - Session + history:
      - appendix/toolkit/library/bytebytego-resource/librarian/sessions/README.md

  - npm shortcuts:
      - updated package.json with:
          - library:librarian:research
          - library:librarian:list
          - library:librarian:checkin

  How it works now

  - Input research question.
  - Selects relevant TOC entries from 2-table-of-contents_ocr.txt.
  - Maps to 3-learnings/<section-id ...>/.
  - Scans images (OCR), writes folder-by-folder notes, builds synthesis answer.
  - Creates/updates ticket LIB-###.
  - Logs CHECKOUT and CHECKIN in history/checkout_log.csv.

  Validated end-to-end

  - Dry run (no OCR): created LIB-002 with session artifacts.
  - OCR run: created LIB-003 with scanned image artifacts and RESOLVED check-in.
  - Example run output folder:
      - appendix/toolkit/library/bytebytego-resource/librarian/sessions/runs/20260221-230833__LIB-003

  Run commands

  npm run library:librarian:list -- --query cache
  npm run library:librarian:research -- --question "How should I reason about caching failures and eviction strategies?"
  npm run library:librarian:checkin -- --ticket-id LIB-003 --status RESOLVED --notes "Done"

  2. If you want a clean seed state, I can archive/remove test tickets LIB-002 and LIB-003 plus their test runs.

• Added appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/README.md (+60 -0)
     1 +# Toolkit Setup Librarian
     2 +
     3 +Internal Use Only.
     4 +
     5 +This is the setup-layer ticket authority for toolkit operations.
     6 +
     7 +It handles:
     8 +1. outgoing tickets (project intake resource selection),
     9 +2. incoming tickets (new resource creation and documentation updates),
    10 +3. review tickets (toolkit retrospective and improvement planning),
    11 +4. check-out/check-in history.
    12 +
    13 +## Why This Exists
    14 +Setup needs a deterministic ticket process before runtime work begins.
    15 +This librarian provides that process in one place under `appendix/toolkit/setup/`.
    16 +
    17 +## Paths
    18 +1. tickets:
    19 +- `appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/tickets/`
    20 +2. templates:
    21 +- `appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/tickets/templates/`
    22 +3. history:
    23 +- `appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/history/ticket_log.csv`
    24 +4. run outputs:
    25 +- `appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/runs/`
    26 +5. operator:
    27 +- `appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/scripts/toolkit_librarian.py`
    28 +
    29 +## Commands
    30 +1. list tickets:
    31 +```bash
    32 +python3 appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/scripts/toolkit_librarian.py list
    33 +```
    34 +2. create outgoing ticket:
    35 +```bash
    36 +python3 appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/scripts/toolkit_librarian.py create \
    37 +  --direction outgoing \
    38 +  --title "Intake: New project setup selection" \
    39 +  --objective "Select authorities, packages, and library sets for bootstrap." \
    40 +  --authorities warden \
    41 +  --packages checker,blueprint,domscan
    42 +```
    43 +3. accept/process ticket:
    44 +```bash
    45 +python3 appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/scripts/toolkit_librarian.py accept --ticket-id TKL-002
    46 +```
    47 +4. check in ticket:
    48 +```bash
    49 +python3 appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/scripts/toolkit_librarian.py checkin \
    50 +  --ticket-id TKL-002 --status RESOLVED --notes "Applied and documented."
    51 +```
    52 +
    53 +## Terminology
    54 +1. `authority`:
    55 +- human+Codex collaboration workspace (for example `warden`, `headmaster`)
    56 +2. `package`:
    57 +- capability module (`checker`, `blueprint`, `domscan`, future modules)
    58 +3. `librarian`:
    59 +- internal setup governance system, not an authority
    60 +

     1 +# Internal Use Policy
     2 +
     3 +Internal Use Only.
     4 +
     5 +This setup librarian is for internal project operations and documentation control.
     6 +
     7 +## Policy
     8 +1. Do not expose these tickets as public product artifacts.
     9 +2. Use tickets to coordinate internal setup decisions only.
    10 +3. Keep implementation evidence local to this repository.
    11 +4. Preserve full ticket history for auditability.
    12 +
    13 +## Authority Boundary
    14 +1. Librarian is an internal setup authority.
    15 +2. Workspace authorities (`warden`, `headmaster`, future authorities) operate project workflows.
    16 +3. Librarian coordinates setup inputs/outputs; it does not replace workspace authorities.
    17 +
    18 +## Ticket Classes
    19 +1. Outgoing
    20 +- select required authorities/packages/library sets for a new target project.
    21 +2. Incoming
    22 +- create or modify a resource (authority, package, library asset) and update docs.
    23 +3. Review
    24 +- evaluate existing toolkit quality and propose improvements.

• Added appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/scripts/toolkit_librarian.py (+560 -0)
      1 +#!/usr/bin/env python3
      2 +"""Toolkit setup librarian ticket operator.
      3 +
      4 +Internal Use Only.
      5 +"""
      6 +
      7 +from __future__ import annotations
      8 +
      9 +import argparse
     10 +import csv
     11 +import json
     12 +import re
     13 +import sys
     14 +from datetime import datetime
     15 +from pathlib import Path
     16 +from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
     17 +
     18 +try:
     19 +    import yaml  # type: ignore
     20 +except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
     21 +    raise SystemExit("Error: PyYAML is required (`pip install pyyaml`).") from exc
     22 +
     23 +
     24 +STATUS_VALUES = {"OPEN", "IN_PROGRESS", "RESOLVED", "BLOCKED"}
     25 +DIRECTION_VALUES = {"outgoing", "incoming", "review"}
     26 +
     27 +
     28 +def _now_iso() -> str:
     29 +    return datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
     30 +
     31 +
     32 +def _now_day() -> str:
     33 +    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
     34 +
     35 +
     36 +def _now_stamp() -> str:
     37 +    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
     38 +
     39 +
     40 +def _split_csv(value: Optional[str]) -> List[str]:
     41 +    if not value:
     42 +        return []
     43 +    return [item.strip() for item in value.split(",") if item.strip()]
     44 +
     45 +
     46 +def librarian_root() -> Path:
     47 +    # .../appendix/toolkit/setup/librarian/scripts/toolkit_librarian.py
     48 +    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
     49 +
     50 +
     51 +def tickets_root() -> Path:
     52 +    return librarian_root() / "tickets"
     53 +
     54 +
     55 +def templates_root() -> Path:
     56 +    return tickets_root() / "templates"
     57 +
     58 +
     59 +def runs_root() -> Path:
     60 +    return librarian_root() / "runs"
     61 +
     62 +
     63 +def history_log_path() -> Path:
     64 +    return librarian_root() / "history" / "ticket_log.csv"
     65 +
     66 +
     67 +def ensure_layout() -> None:
     68 +    tickets_root().mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
     69 +    templates_root().mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
     70 +    runs_root().mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
     71 +    history_log_path().parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
     72 +    if not history_log_path().exists():
     73 +        history_log_path().write_text(
     74 +            "timestamp,event,ticket_id,status,actor,direction,title,run_dir,notes\n",
     75 +            encoding="utf-8",
     76 +        )
     77 +
     78 +
     79 +def _ticket_sort_key(path: Path) -> tuple[int, str]:
     80 +    match = re.match(r"TKL-(\d{3})", path.stem)
     81 +    if match:
     82 +        return (int(match.group(1)), path.stem)
     83 +    return (999999, path.stem)
     84 +
     85 +
     86 +def ticket_file_candidates() -> List[Path]:
     87 +    files = [p for p in tickets_root().glob("*.yaml") if p.is_file()]
     88 +    files = [p for p in files if p.parent.name != "templates"]
     89 +    files.sort(key=_ticket_sort_key)
     90 +    return files
     91 +
     92 +
     93 +def load_ticket(ticket_id: str) -> tuple[Path, Dict[str, Any]]:
     94 +    candidates = [
     95 +        tickets_root() / f"{ticket_id}.yaml",
     96 +    ]
     97 +    matches = list(tickets_root().glob(f"{ticket_id}-*.yaml"))
     98 +    candidates.extend(matches)
     99 +
    100 +    existing = [p for p in candidates if p.exists()]
    101 +    if not existing:
    102 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Ticket not found: {ticket_id}")
    103 +    path = existing[0]
    104 +    data = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    105 +    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    106 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid ticket YAML object in: {path}")
    107 +    return path, data
    108 +
    109 +
    110 +def write_ticket(path: Path, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    111 +    path.write_text(
    112 +        yaml.safe_dump(
    113 +            data,
    114 +            sort_keys=False,
    115 +            allow_unicode=False,
    116 +            default_flow_style=False,
    117 +        ),
    118 +        encoding="utf-8",
    119 +    )
    120 +
    121 +
    122 +def next_ticket_id() -> str:
    123 +    max_id = 0
    124 +    for file in ticket_file_candidates():
    125 +        match = re.match(r"TKL-(\d{3})", file.stem)
    126 +        if match:
    127 +            max_id = max(max_id, int(match.group(1)))
    128 +    return f"TKL-{max_id + 1:03d}"
    129 +
    130 +
    131 +def append_history(
    132 +    event: str,
    133 +    ticket_id: str,
    134 +    status: str,
    135 +    actor: str,
    136 +    direction: str,
    137 +    title: str,
    138 +    run_dir: Path,
    139 +    notes: str,
    140 +) -> None:
    141 +    with history_log_path().open("a", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
    142 +        writer = csv.writer(handle)
    143 +        writer.writerow(
    144 +            [
    145 +                _now_iso(),
    146 +                event,
    147 +                ticket_id,
    148 +                status,
    149 +                actor,
    150 +                direction,
    151 +                title.replace("\n", " ").strip(),
    152 +                str(run_dir),
    153 +                notes.replace("\n", " ").strip(),
    154 +            ]
    155 +        )
    156 +
    157 +
    158 +def resolve_direction(ticket: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    159 +    direction = str(ticket.get("direction", "")).strip().lower()
    160 +    if direction in DIRECTION_VALUES:
    161 +        return direction
    162 +    ticket_type = str(ticket.get("ticket_type", "")).strip().lower()
    163 +    if "outgoing" in ticket_type or "intake" in ticket_type:
    164 +        return "outgoing"
    165 +    if "incoming" in ticket_type or "resource" in ticket_type:
    166 +        return "incoming"
    167 +    return "review"
    168 +
    169 +
    170 +def _template_name_for(direction: str) -> str:
    171 +    if direction == "outgoing":
    172 +        return "outgoing_ticket_template.yaml"
    173 +    if direction == "incoming":
    174 +        return "incoming_ticket_template.yaml"
    175 +    return "review_ticket_template.yaml"
    176 +
    177 +
    178 +def _load_template(direction: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    179 +    template_file = templates_root() / _template_name_for(direction)
    180 +    if not template_file.exists():
    181 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Template missing: {template_file}")
    182 +    payload = yaml.safe_load(template_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    183 +    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    184 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid template object: {template_file}")
    185 +    return payload
    186 +
    187 +
    188 +def _build_ticket_from_args(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    189 +    direction = args.direction.lower().strip()
    190 +    if direction not in DIRECTION_VALUES:
    191 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid direction: {direction}")
    192 +
    193 +    ticket = _load_template(direction)
    194 +    ticket_id = args.ticket_id or next_ticket_id()
    195 +    ticket["ticket_id"] = ticket_id
    196 +    ticket["date"] = _now_day()
    197 +    ticket["status"] = "OPEN"
    198 +    ticket["direction"] = direction
    199 +    ticket["title"] = args.title
    200 +    ticket["objective"] = args.objective
    201 +    ticket["internal_use_only"] = True
    202 +
    203 +    if direction == "outgoing":
    204 +        target = ticket.setdefault("target_project", {})
    205 +        target["name"] = args.target_project_name or ""
    206 +        target["path"] = args.target_project_path or ""
    207 +
    208 +        selection = ticket.setdefault("resource_selection", {})
    209 +        selection["authorities"] = _split_csv(args.authorities)
    210 +        selection["packages"] = _split_csv(args.packages)
    211 +        selection["library_sets"] = _split_csv(args.library_sets)
    212 +        if args.scan_package_label:
    213 +            selection["scan_package_label"] = args.scan_package_label
    214 +
    215 +    elif direction == "incoming":
    216 +        incoming = ticket.setdefault("incoming_resource", {})
    217 +        if args.resource_kind:
    218 +            incoming["resource_kind"] = args.resource_kind
    219 +        incoming["resource_name"] = args.resource_name or ""
    220 +        incoming["summary"] = args.resource_summary or ""
    221 +
    222 +    else:
    223 +        scope = ticket.setdefault("review_scope", {})
    224 +        if args.review_all_off:
    225 +            scope["include_setup_layer"] = False
    226 +            scope["include_authority_layer"] = False
    227 +            scope["include_packages_layer"] = False
    228 +            scope["include_library_layer"] = False
    229 +        if args.review_setup:
    230 +            scope["include_setup_layer"] = True
    231 +        if args.review_authority:
    232 +            scope["include_authority_layer"] = True
    233 +        if args.review_packages:
    234 +            scope["include_packages_layer"] = True
    235 +        if args.review_library:
    236 +            scope["include_library_layer"] = True
    237 +
    238 +    return ticket
    239 +
    240 +
    241 +def _write_outgoing_plan(run_dir: Path, ticket: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    242 +    selection = ticket.get("resource_selection", {}) or {}
    243 +    target = ticket.get("target_project", {}) or {}
    244 +    authorities = selection.get("authorities", []) or []
    245 +    packages = selection.get("packages", []) or []
    246 +    library_sets = selection.get("library_sets", []) or []
    247 +    scan_package_label = selection.get("scan_package_label", "domscan")
    248 +
    249 +    authority = authorities[0] if authorities else "warden"
    250 +    lines = []
    251 +    lines.append("# Outgoing Setup Plan")
    252 +    lines.append("")
    253 +    lines.append("Internal Use Only.")
    254 +    lines.append("")
    255 +    lines.append(f"- ticket_id: {ticket.get('ticket_id', '')}")
    256 +    lines.append(f"- target_project_name: {target.get('name', '')}")
    257 +    lines.append(f"- target_project_path: {target.get('path', '')}")
    258 +    lines.append("")
    259 +    lines.append("## Selected Resources")
    260 +    lines.append(f"- authorities: {', '.join(authorities) if authorities else '(not set)'}")
    261 +    lines.append(f"- packages: {', '.join(packages) if packages else '(not set)'}")
    262 +    lines.append(f"- library_sets: {', '.join(library_sets) if library_sets else '(not set)'}")
    263 +    lines.append(f"- scan_package_label: {scan_package_label}")
    264 +    lines.append("")
    265 +    lines.append("## Bootstrap Commands")
    266 +    lines.append(
    267 +        f"- bash appendix/toolkit/setup/master_operator.sh --workspace {authority} --scan-package {scan_package_label} --mode plan"
    268 +    )
    269 +    lines.append(
    270 +        f"- bash appendix/toolkit/setup/master_operator.sh --workspace {authority} --scan-package {scan_package_label} --mode apply"
    271 +    )
    272 +    lines.append("")
    273 +    lines.append("## Documentation Delta")
    274 +    lines.append("- verify `appendix/toolkit/DEFAULT_FACTORY_SETTINGS.md` alignment")
    275 +    lines.append("- verify `appendix/toolkit/PROCESS.md` authority/package mapping")
    276 +    lines.append("- verify `appendix/toolkit/IMPLEMENTATION.md` reflects applied selection")
    277 +    lines.append("- verify workspace authority docs under `appendix/toolkit/workspace/`")
    278 +    (run_dir / "outgoing_plan.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    279 +
    280 +
    281 +def _incoming_path_map(resource_kind: str, resource_name: str) -> List[str]:
    282 +    resource_name = resource_name.strip()
    283 +    if not resource_name:
    284 +        resource_name = "<resource-name>"
    285 +    if resource_kind == "authority":
    286 +        return [
    287 +            f"appendix/toolkit/workspace/{resource_name}/README.md",
    288 +            f"appendix/toolkit/setup/templates/workspace/{resource_name}/",
    289 +            "appendix/toolkit/PROCESS.md",
    290 +            "appendix/toolkit/DEFAULT_FACTORY_SETTINGS.md",
    291 +        ]
    292 +    if resource_kind == "library":
    293 +        return [
    294 +            f"appendix/toolkit/library/{resource_name}/",
    295 +            "appendix/toolkit/library/README.md",
    296 +            "appendix/toolkit/PROCESS.md",
    297 +            "appendix/toolkit/IMPLEMENTATION.md",
    298 +        ]
    299 +    if resource_kind == "setup-script":
    300 +        return [
    301 +            f"appendix/toolkit/setup/{resource_name}",
    302 +            "appendix/toolkit/setup/README.md",
    303 +            "appendix/toolkit/setup/MASTER_OPERATOR.md",
    304 +            "appendix/toolkit/IMPLEMENTATION.md",
    305 +        ]
    306 +    return [
    307 +        f"appendix/toolkit/packages/{resource_name}/PACKAGE.md",
    308 +        "appendix/toolkit/packages/README.md",
    309 +        "package.json",
    310 +        "appendix/toolkit/PROCESS.md",
    311 +        "appendix/toolkit/IMPLEMENTATION.md",
    312 +    ]
    313 +
    314 +
    315 +def _write_incoming_plan(run_dir: Path, ticket: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    316 +    incoming = ticket.get("incoming_resource", {}) or {}
    317 +    change = ticket.get("change_requirements", {}) or {}
    318 +    kind = str(incoming.get("resource_kind", "package"))
    319 +    name = str(incoming.get("resource_name", ""))
    320 +    summary = str(incoming.get("summary", ""))
    321 +    path_map = _incoming_path_map(kind, name)
    322 +
    323 +    lines = []
    324 +    lines.append("# Incoming Resource Creation Plan")
    325 +    lines.append("")
    326 +    lines.append("Internal Use Only.")
    327 +    lines.append("")
    328 +    lines.append(f"- ticket_id: {ticket.get('ticket_id', '')}")
    329 +    lines.append(f"- resource_kind: {kind}")
    330 +    lines.append(f"- resource_name: {name if name else '(not set)'}")
    331 +    lines.append(f"- summary: {summary if summary else '(not set)'}")
    332 +    lines.append("")
    333 +    lines.append("## Required File Path Map")
    334 +    for path in path_map:
    335 +        lines.append(f"- `{path}`")
    336 +    lines.append("")
    337 +    lines.append("## Required Change Flags")
    338 +    for key, value in change.items():
    339 +        lines.append(f"- {key}: {value}")
    340 +    lines.append("")
    341 +    lines.append("## Implementation Notes")
    342 +    lines.append("- update implementation docs after resource creation")
    343 +    lines.append("- keep authority terminology consistent (`authority`, not `agent`)")
    344 +    lines.append("- keep internal-only notice in setup librarian docs")
    345 +    (run_dir / "incoming_plan.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    346 +
    347 +
    348 +def _write_review_plan(run_dir: Path, ticket: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    349 +    scope = ticket.get("review_scope", {}) or {}
    350 +    lines = []
    351 +    lines.append("# Toolkit Review Plan")
    352 +    lines.append("")
    353 +    lines.append("Internal Use Only.")
    354 +    lines.append("")
    355 +    lines.append(f"- ticket_id: {ticket.get('ticket_id', '')}")
    356 +    lines.append("")
    357 +    lines.append("## Scope")
    358 +    for key in [
    359 +        "include_setup_layer",
    360 +        "include_authority_layer",
    361 +        "include_packages_layer",
    362 +        "include_library_layer",
    363 +    ]:
    364 +        lines.append(f"- {key}: {scope.get(key, False)}")
    365 +    lines.append("")
    366 +    lines.append("## Review Checklist")
    367 +    lines.append("- setup scripts reliability (`master_operator.sh`, `scout_project.sh`, `factory_reset.sh`)")
    368 +    lines.append("- authority template clarity (`appendix/toolkit/workspace/*`)")
    369 +    lines.append("- package contract usability (`appendix/toolkit/packages/*/PACKAGE.md`)")
    370 +    lines.append("- library governance (`appendix/toolkit/library/README.md`)")
    371 +    lines.append("- improvement backlog with impact/effort ranking")
    372 +    (run_dir / "review_plan.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    373 +
    374 +
    375 +def _write_run_manifest(run_dir: Path, ticket_path: Path, ticket: Dict[str, Any], actor: str) -> None:
    376 +    payload = {
    377 +        "timestamp": _now_iso(),
    378 +        "ticket_id": ticket.get("ticket_id", ""),
    379 +        "ticket_path": str(ticket_path),
    380 +        "direction": resolve_direction(ticket),
    381 +        "status": ticket.get("status", ""),
    382 +        "actor": actor,
    383 +        "run_dir": str(run_dir),
    384 +    }
    385 +    (run_dir / "run_manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    386 +
    387 +
    388 +def cmd_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    389 +    ensure_layout()
    390 +    ticket = _build_ticket_from_args(args)
    391 +    ticket_id = str(ticket["ticket_id"])
    392 +    out_path = tickets_root() / f"{ticket_id}.yaml"
    393 +    if out_path.exists() and not args.force:
    394 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Ticket already exists: {out_path} (use --force to overwrite)")
    395 +    write_ticket(out_path, ticket)
    396 +    print(f"created={out_path}")
    397 +    return 0
    398 +
    399 +
    400 +def cmd_list(_: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    401 +    ensure_layout()
    402 +    print("ticket_id | status       | direction | title")
    403 +    print("-" * 100)
    404 +    for file in ticket_file_candidates():
    405 +        data = yaml.safe_load(file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    406 +        ticket_id = str(data.get("ticket_id", file.stem))
    407 +        status = str(data.get("status", ""))
    408 +        direction = str(resolve_direction(data))
    409 +        title = str(data.get("title", ""))
    410 +        print(f"{ticket_id:8} | {status:12} | {direction:9} | {title}")
    411 +    return 0
    412 +
    413 +
    414 +def cmd_accept(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    415 +    ensure_layout()
    416 +    ticket_path, ticket = load_ticket(args.ticket_id)
    417 +    ticket_id = str(ticket.get("ticket_id", args.ticket_id))
    418 +    direction = resolve_direction(ticket)
    419 +    title = str(ticket.get("title", ""))
    420 +
    421 +    ticket["status"] = "IN_PROGRESS"
    422 +    write_ticket(ticket_path, ticket)
    423 +
    424 +    run_dir = runs_root() / f"{_now_stamp()}__{ticket_id}"
    425 +    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    426 +
    427 +    append_history(
    428 +        event="CHECKOUT",
    429 +        ticket_id=ticket_id,
    430 +        status="IN_PROGRESS",
    431 +        actor=args.actor,
    432 +        direction=direction,
    433 +        title=title,
    434 +        run_dir=run_dir,
    435 +        notes=args.notes or "",
    436 +    )
    437 +
    438 +    if direction == "outgoing":
    439 +        _write_outgoing_plan(run_dir, ticket)
    440 +    elif direction == "incoming":
    441 +        _write_incoming_plan(run_dir, ticket)
    442 +    else:
    443 +        _write_review_plan(run_dir, ticket)
    444 +
    445 +    _write_run_manifest(run_dir, ticket_path, ticket, args.actor)
    446 +
    447 +    if args.auto_checkin_status:
    448 +        status = args.auto_checkin_status.upper()
    449 +        if status not in STATUS_VALUES:
    450 +            raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid auto checkin status: {status}")
    451 +        ticket["status"] = status
    452 +        write_ticket(ticket_path, ticket)
    453 +        append_history(
    454 +            event="CHECKIN",
    455 +            ticket_id=ticket_id,
    456 +            status=status,
    457 +            actor=args.actor,
    458 +            direction=direction,
    459 +            title=title,
    460 +            run_dir=run_dir,
    461 +            notes=args.notes or "",
    462 +        )
    463 +
    464 +    print(f"ticket_id={ticket_id}")
    465 +    print(f"direction={direction}")
    466 +    print(f"run_dir={run_dir}")
    467 +    print(f"status={ticket.get('status', '')}")
    468 +    return 0
    469 +
    470 +
    471 +def cmd_checkin(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    472 +    ensure_layout()
    473 +    ticket_path, ticket = load_ticket(args.ticket_id)
    474 +    ticket_id = str(ticket.get("ticket_id", args.ticket_id))
    475 +    status = args.status.upper()
    476 +    if status not in STATUS_VALUES:
    477 +        raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid status: {status}")
    478 +    direction = resolve_direction(ticket)
    479 +    title = str(ticket.get("title", ""))
    480 +    run_dir = Path(args.run_dir) if args.run_dir else runs_root()
    481 +
    482 +    ticket["status"] = status
    483 +    write_ticket(ticket_path, ticket)
    484 +    append_history(
    485 +        event="CHECKIN",
    486 +        ticket_id=ticket_id,
    487 +        status=status,
    488 +        actor=args.actor,
    489 +        direction=direction,
    490 +        title=title,
    491 +        run_dir=run_dir,
    492 +        notes=args.notes or "",
    493 +    )
    494 +    print(f"ticket_id={ticket_id}")
    495 +    print(f"status={status}")
    496 +    return 0
    497 +
    498 +
    499 +def make_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    500 +    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Toolkit setup librarian ticket operator (internal use only)")
    501 +    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    502 +
    503 +    create = sub.add_parser("create", help="Create a new librarian ticket")
    504 +    create.add_argument("--direction", required=True, choices=sorted(DIRECTION_VALUES))
    505 +    create.add_argument("--ticket-id", help="Optional explicit ticket ID (e.g., TKL-020)")
    506 +    create.add_argument("--title", required=True)
    507 +    create.add_argument("--objective", required=True)
    508 +    create.add_argument("--target-project-name")
    509 +    create.add_argument("--target-project-path")
    510 +    create.add_argument("--authorities", help="Comma-separated authorities for outgoing tickets")
    511 +    create.add_argument("--packages", help="Comma-separated packages for outgoing tickets")
    512 +    create.add_argument("--library-sets", help="Comma-separated library sets for outgoing tickets")
    513 +    create.add_argument("--scan-package-label")
    514 +    create.add_argument("--resource-kind", help="incoming: authority|package|library|setup-script")
    515 +    create.add_argument("--resource-name", help="incoming: resource name")
    516 +    create.add_argument("--resource-summary", help="incoming: summary")
    517 +    create.add_argument("--review-all-off", action="store_true", help="review: disable all scopes first")
    518 +    create.add_argument("--review-setup", action="store_true")
    519 +    create.add_argument("--review-authority", action="store_true")
    520 +    create.add_argument("--review-packages", action="store_true")
    521 +    create.add_argument("--review-library", action="store_true")
    522 +    create.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="Overwrite ticket file if it already exists")
    523 +    create.set_defaults(fn=cmd_create)
    524 +
    525 +    listing = sub.add_parser("list", help="List setup librarian tickets")
    526 +    listing.set_defaults(fn=cmd_list)
    527 +
    528 +    accept = sub.add_parser("accept", help="Accept/process ticket and generate run artifacts")
    529 +    accept.add_argument("--ticket-id", required=True)
    530 +    accept.add_argument("--actor", default="codex")
    531 +    accept.add_argument("--notes")
    532 +    accept.add_argument(
    533 +        "--auto-checkin-status",
    534 +        help="Optional status to auto checkin after processing (OPEN|IN_PROGRESS|RESOLVED|BLOCKED)",
    535 +    )
    536 +    accept.set_defaults(fn=cmd_accept)
    537 +
    538 +    checkin = sub.add_parser("checkin", help="Check in ticket with status update")
    539 +    checkin.add_argument("--ticket-id", required=True)
    540 +    checkin.add_argument("--status", required=True, help="OPEN|IN_PROGRESS|RESOLVED|BLOCKED")
    541 +    checkin.add_argument("--actor", default="codex")
    542 +    checkin.add_argument("--run-dir", help="Optional run dir path to log")
    543 +    checkin.add_argument("--notes")
    544 +    checkin.set_defaults(fn=cmd_checkin)
    545 +
    546 +    return parser
    547 +
    548 +
    549 +def main() -> int:
    550 +    args = make_parser().parse_args()
    551 +    try:
    552 +        return args.fn(args)
    553 +    except Exception as exc:
    554 +        print(f"Error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
    555 +        return 1
    556 +
    557 +
    558 +if __name__ == "__main__":
    559 +    raise SystemExit(main())
    560 +

    355 +def _as_list(value: Any) -> List[str]:

    363 +def _classify_file(path: Path) -> str:
    364 +    suffix = path.suffix.lower()
    365 +    name = path.name.lower()
    366 +    parts = {part.lower() for part in path.parts}
    367 +    if suffix in {".md", ".txt", ".rst", ".adoc"} or "readme" in name:
    368 +        return "docs"
    369 +    if suffix in {".sh", ".py", ".js", ".ts", ".tsx", ".mjs", ".cjs"}:
    370 +        return "scripts"
    371 +    if suffix in {".yaml", ".yml", ".json", ".toml", ".ini", ".conf"}:
    372 +        return "configs"
    373 +    if "tests" in parts or "test" in parts or name.startswith("test_") or name.endswith("_test.py"):
    374 +        return "tests"
    375 +    if suffix in {".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".svg", ".gif", ".webp"}:
    376 +        return "assets"
    377 +    return "other"

    413 +def _scan_source(source_root: Path, globs: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    414 +    exists = source_root.exists() and source_root.is_dir()
    415 +    snapshot: Dict[str, Any] = {
    416 +        "source_root": str(source_root),
    417 +        "exists": exists,
    418 +        "dir_count": 0,
    419 +        "file_count": 0,
    420 +        "top_entries": [],
    421 +        "matched_files": [],
    422 +        "category_counts": {"docs": 0, "scripts": 0, "configs": 0, "tests": 0, "assets": 0, "other": 0},
    423 +        "category_examples": {"docs": [], "scripts": [], "configs": [], "tests": [], "assets": [], "other": []},
    424 +        "detected_signals": [],
    425 +    }
    426 +
    427 +    if not exists:
    428 +        return snapshot
    429 +
    430 +    top_entries = []
    431 +    for entry in sorted(source_root.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower())[:MAX_CAPTURED_FILES]:
    432 +        suffix = "/" if entry.is_dir() else ""
    433 +        top_entries.append(f"{entry.name}{suffix}")
    434 +    snapshot["top_entries"] = top_entries
    435 +
    436 +    matched_files: List[str] = []
    437 +    signals: set[str] = set()
    438 +
    439 +    for walk_root, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(source_root):
    440 +        snapshot["dir_count"] += len(dirnames)
    441 +        walk_path = Path(walk_root)
    442 +        for filename in filenames:
    443 +            file_path = walk_path / filename
    444 +            rel = file_path.relative_to(source_root).as_posix()
    445 +            snapshot["file_count"] += 1
    446 +
    447 +            category = _classify_file(file_path)
    448 +            snapshot["category_counts"][category] += 1
    449 +            examples = snapshot["category_examples"][category]
    450 +            if len(examples) < 8:
    451 +                examples.append(rel)
    452 +
    453 +            if any(fnmatch.fnmatch(rel, pattern) for pattern in globs):
    454 +                if len(matched_files) < MAX_CAPTURED_FILES:
    455 +                    matched_files.append(rel)
    456 +
    457 +            rel_lower = rel.lower()
    458 +            name_lower = filename.lower()
    459 +            if name_lower == "package.json":
    460 +                signals.add("Node package surface detected (`package.json`).")
    461 +            if name_lower in {"pyproject.toml", "requirements.txt"}:
    462 +                signals.add("Python package surface detected.")
    463 +            if rel_lower.startswith("docs/") or name_lower.startswith("readme"):
    464 +                signals.add("Documentation surface detected.")
    465 +            if "/tickets/" in rel_lower or rel_lower.endswith("_ticket_template.yaml"):
    466 +                signals.add("Ticket governance patterns detected.")
    467 +            if "/scripts/" in rel_lower:
    468 +                signals.add("Automation script surface detected.")
    469 +
    470 +    snapshot["matched_files"] = matched_files
    471 +    snapshot["detected_signals"] = sorted(signals)
    472 +    return snapshot
    473 +

• Added appendix/toolkit/toolkit.sh (+83 -0)
     1 +#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2 +set -euo pipefail
     3 +
     4 +SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     5 +TOOLKIT_ROOT="$SCRIPT_DIR"
     6 +
     7 +usage() {
     8 +  cat <<USAGE
     9 +Toolkit Standalone Entrypoint
    10 +
    11 +Usage:
    12 +  bash /path/to/toolkit/toolkit.sh <command> [args]
    13 +
    14 +Commands:
    15 +  scout <project_root> [workspace] [scan_package]
    16 +  plan  --project-root <project_root> [master_operator args...]
    17 +  apply --project-root <project_root> [master_operator args...]
    18 +  reset [factory_reset args...]
    19 +  librarian [toolkit_librarian args...]
    20 +  help
    21 +
    22 +Examples:
    23 +  bash /path/to/toolkit/toolkit.sh scout /path/to/project warden domscan
    24 +  bash /path/to/toolkit/toolkit.sh plan --project-root /path/to/project --workspace warden --scan-package domscan
    25 +  bash /path/to/toolkit/toolkit.sh apply --project-root /path/to/project --workspace warden
    26 +  bash /path/to/toolkit/toolkit.sh reset --project-root /path/to/project --workspace warden --level workspace-runtime
    27 +  bash /path/to/toolkit/toolkit.sh librarian list
    28 +USAGE
    29 +}
    30 +
    31 +has_project_root_arg() {
    32 +  local previous=""
    33 +  for token in "$@"; do
    34 +    if [ "$previous" = "--project-root" ]; then
    35 +      [ -n "$token" ] && return 0
    36 +    fi
    37 +    previous="$token"
    38 +  done
    39 +  return 1
    40 +}
    41 +
    42 +command_name="${1:-help}"
    43 +if [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; then
    44 +  shift
    45 +fi
    46 +
    47 +case "$command_name" in
    48 +  scout)
    49 +    project_root="${1:-$(pwd)}"
    50 +    workspace="${2:-warden}"
    51 +    scan_package="${3:-domscan}"
    52 +    bash "$TOOLKIT_ROOT/setup/scout_project.sh" "$project_root" "$workspace" "$scan_package"
    53 +    ;;
    54 +  plan)
    55 +    if ! has_project_root_arg "$@"; then
    56 +      echo "Error: --project-root is required for plan." >&2
    57 +      exit 1
    58 +    fi
    59 +    bash "$TOOLKIT_ROOT/setup/master_operator.sh" --mode plan "$@"
    60 +    ;;
    61 +  apply)
    62 +    if ! has_project_root_arg "$@"; then
    63 +      echo "Error: --project-root is required for apply." >&2
    64 +      exit 1
    65 +    fi
    66 +    bash "$TOOLKIT_ROOT/setup/master_operator.sh" --mode apply "$@"
    67 +    ;;
    68 +  reset)
    69 +    bash "$TOOLKIT_ROOT/setup/factory_reset.sh" "$@"
    70 +    ;;
    71 +  librarian)
    72 +    python3 "$TOOLKIT_ROOT/setup/librarian/scripts/toolkit_librarian.py" "$@"
    73 +    ;;
    74 +  help|--help|-h)
    75 +    usage
    76 +    ;;
    77 +  *)
    78 +    echo "Unknown command: $command_name" >&2
    79 +    usage
    80 +    exit 1
    81 +    ;;
    82 +esac
    83 +
