    100 +    "LandingAI": "ai_foundation_models",
    101 +    "Tavily": "search_research",
    102 +    "Exa": "search_research",
    103 +    "Serper": "search_research",
    104 +    "SerpAPI": "search_research",
    105 +    "Brave": "search_research",
    106 +    "DuckDuckGo": "search_research",
    107 +    "GitHub": "platform_devops",
    108 +    "Railway": "platform_devops",
    109 +    "Polygon": "market_data",
    110 +    "LangChain": "observability_eval",
    111 +    "Dropbox": "storage_content",
    112 +    "YouTube": "general_api_access",
    113 +}
    114 +
    115 +
    116 +def now_iso() -> str:
    117 +    return datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    118 +
    119 +
    120 +def now_stamp() -> str:
    121 +    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    122 +
    123 +
    124 +def parse_sources(raw_sources: List[str]) -> List[SourceProject]:
    125 +    if not raw_sources:
    126 +        return DEFAULT_SOURCES
    127 +    parsed: List[SourceProject] = []
    128 +    for item in raw_sources:
    129 +        if "=" not in item:
    130 +            raise ValueError(f"Invalid --source format (expected label=path): {item}")
    131 +        label, raw_path = item.split("=", 1)
    132 +        label = label.strip()
    133 +        path = Path(raw_path.strip()).expanduser()
    134 +        if not label:
    135 +            raise ValueError(f"Invalid source label in: {item}")
    136 +        parsed.append(SourceProject(label, path))
    137 +    return parsed
    138 +
    139 +
    140 +def extract_env_keys(project_root: Path) -> List[str]:
    141 +    keys = set()
    142 +    for filename in (".env", ".env.example"):
    143 +        env_path = project_root / filename
    144 +        if not env_path.exists():
    145 +            continue
    146 +        text = env_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
    147 +        for line in text.splitlines():
    148 +            line = line.strip()
    149 +            if not line or line.startswith("#"):
    150 +                continue
    151 +            match = re.match(r"([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)=", line)
    152 +            if match:
    153 +                keys.add(match.group(1))
    154 +    return sorted(keys)
    155 +
    156 +
    157 +def read_api_catalog_providers(catalog_path: Path, source_label: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    158 +    if not catalog_path.exists():
    159 +        return []
    160 +    payload = json.loads(catalog_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    161 +    providers = payload.get("providers", [])
    162 +    cleaned: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    163 +    for item in providers:
    164 +        if not isinstance(item, dict):
    165 +            continue
    166 +        provider = str(item.get("provider", "")).strip()
    167 +        product = str(item.get("product", "")).strip()
    168 +        if not provider and not product:
    169 +            continue
    170 +        env_vars_raw = item.get("env_vars", [])
    171 +        env_vars = [x for x in env_vars_raw if isinstance(x, str)]
    172 +        cleaned.append(
    173 +            {
    174 +                "provider": provider,
    175 +                "product": product,
    176 +                "status": str(item.get("status", "UNKNOWN")),
    177 +                "env_vars": sorted(set(env_vars)),
    178 +                "base_url": str(item.get("base_url", "")),
    179 +                "offers": [x for x in item.get("offers", []) if isinstance(x, str)],
    180 +                "best_for": [x for x in item.get("best_for", []) if isinstance(x, str)],
    181 +                "sources": [source_label],
    182 +            }
    183 +        )
    184 +    return cleaned
    185 +
    186 +
    187 +def extract_polygon_provider(project_root: Path, source_label: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    188 +    manager_path = project_root / "data-center" / "pnf_data_manager.py"
    189 +    if not manager_path.exists():
    190 +        return []
    191 +    text = manager_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
    192 +    base_url_match = re.search(r'BASE_URL\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"', text)
    193 +    if not base_url_match:
    194 +        return []
    195 +    base_url = base_url_match.group(1)
    196 +    if "polygon.io" not in base_url:
    197 +        return []
    198 +    return [
    199 +        {
    200 +            "provider": "Polygon",
    201 +            "product": "Aggregates API",
    202 +            "status": "ACTIVE",
    203 +            "env_vars": ["POLYGON_API_KEY"],
    204 +            "base_url": "https://api.polygon.io",
    205 +            "offers": ["OHLC aggregates", "ticker range data", "historical fetch for chart engine"],
    206 +            "best_for": ["market data ingestion", "chart precomputation"],
    207 +            "sources": [source_label],
    208 +        }
    209 +    ]
    210 +
    211 +
    212 +def infer_additional_providers(env_keys: Iterable[str], source_label: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    213 +    keys = set(env_keys)
    214 +    providers: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    215 +
    216 +    def add(provider: str, product: str, required: List[str], status: str = "ACTIVE") -> None:
    217 +        providers.append(
    218 +            {
    219 +                "provider": provider,
    220 +                "product": product,
    221 +                "status": status,
    222 +                "env_vars": required,
    223 +                "base_url": PROVIDER_BASE_URLS.get(provider, ""),
    224 +                "offers": [],
    225 +                "best_for": [],
    226 +                "sources": [source_label],
    227 +            }
    228 +        )
    229 +
    230 +    if "PAYPAL_ENVIRONMENT" in keys:
    231 +        add(
    232 +            "PayPal",
    233 +            "Checkout + OAuth",
    234 +            [
    235 +                "PAYPAL_ENVIRONMENT",
    236 +                "PAYPAL_SANDBOX_CLIENT_ID",
    237 +                "PAYPAL_SANDBOX_CLIENT_SECRET",
    238 +                "PAYPAL_LIVE_CLIENT_ID",
    239 +                "PAYPAL_LIVE_CLIENT_SECRET",
    240 +                "PAYPAL_WEBHOOK_ID",
    241 +            ],
    242 +        )
    243 +    if "CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN" in keys:
    244 +        add("Cloudflare", "DNS + Zone API", ["CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN", "CLOUDFLARE_ZONE_ID", "CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID"])
    245 +    if "RESEND_API_KEY" in keys:
    246 +        add("Resend", "Domains + Email API", ["RESEND_API_KEY", "RESEND_DOMAIN"])
    247 +    if "RAILWAY_TOKEN" in keys:
    248 +        add("Railway", "Service Variables + Deployments", ["RAILWAY_TOKEN"])
    249 +    if "VOLET_API_KEY" in keys:
    250 +        add("Volet", "Merchant Payments API", ["VOLET_API_KEY", "VOLET_MERCHANT_ID", "VOLET_ENVIRONMENT"])
    251 +    if "GA_MEASUREMENT_ID" in keys:
    252 +        add("Google Analytics", "Measurement ID", ["GA_MEASUREMENT_ID"])
    253 +    return providers
    254 +
    255 +
    256 +def provider_key(provider: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    257 +    return (provider.get("provider", "").strip().lower(), provider.get("product", "").strip().lower())
    258 +
    259 +
    260 +def merge_providers(records: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    261 +    merged: Dict[Tuple[str, str], Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    262 +    for rec in records:
    263 +        key = provider_key(rec)
    264 +        if key not in merged:
    265 +            merged[key] = {
    266 +                "provider": rec.get("provider", ""),
    267 +                "product": rec.get("product", ""),
    268 +                "status": rec.get("status", "UNKNOWN"),
    269 +                "env_vars": set(rec.get("env_vars", [])),
    270 +                "base_url": rec.get("base_url", ""),
    271 +                "offers": set(rec.get("offers", [])),
    272 +                "best_for": set(rec.get("best_for", [])),
    273 +                "sources": set(rec.get("sources", [])),
    274 +            }
    275 +            continue
    276 +        dst = merged[key]
    277 +        dst["env_vars"].update(rec.get("env_vars", []))
    278 +        dst["offers"].update(rec.get("offers", []))
    279 +        dst["best_for"].update(rec.get("best_for", []))
    280 +        dst["sources"].update(rec.get("sources", []))
    281 +        if not dst["base_url"] and rec.get("base_url"):
    282 +            dst["base_url"] = rec.get("base_url")
    283 +        status = str(rec.get("status", "UNKNOWN")).upper()
    284 +        if dst["status"] != "ACTIVE" and status == "ACTIVE":
    285 +            dst["status"] = "ACTIVE"
    286 +
    287 +    normalized: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    288 +    for item in merged.values():
    289 +        normalized.append(
    290 +            {
    291 +                "provider": item["provider"],
    292 +                "product": item["product"],
    293 +                "status": item["status"],
    294 +                "env_vars": sorted(item["env_vars"]),
    295 +                "base_url": item["base_url"],
    296 +                "offers": sorted(item["offers"]),
    297 +                "best_for": sorted(item["best_for"]),
    298 +                "sources": sorted(item["sources"]),
    299 +            }
    300 +        )
    301 +    normalized.sort(key=lambda x: (x["provider"], x["product"]))
    302 +    return normalized
    303 +
    304 +
    305 +def sector_for_provider(provider: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    306 +    provider_name = provider.get("provider", "")
    307 +    product_name = provider.get("product", "")
    308 +    key_blob = f"{provider_name} {product_name}".lower()
    309 +    for hint, sector in PROVIDER_SECTOR_HINTS.items():
    310 +        if hint.lower() in key_blob:
    311 +            return sector
    312 +
    313 +    env_vars = provider.get("env_vars", [])
    314 +    if any(str(key).startswith("AWS_") for key in env_vars):
    315 +        return "cloud_aws"
    316 +    if any(str(key).startswith(("GOOGLE_", "VERTEX_", "GSC_")) for key in env_vars):
    317 +        return "cloud_gcp"
    318 +    return "general_api_access"
    319 +
    320 +
    321 +def extract_models_from_file(script_path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    322 +    if not script_path.exists():
    323 +        return []
    324 +    text = script_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
    325 +    results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    326 +    for match in re.finditer(r"([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*\[(.*?)\]", text, flags=re.DOTALL):
    327 +        var_name = match.group(1)
    328 +        body = match.group(2)
    329 +        model_ids = re.findall(r'"([^"]+)"', body)
    330 +        if not model_ids:
    331 +            continue
    332 +        provider = "Unknown"
    333 +        if "OPENROUTER" in var_name:
    334 +            provider = "OpenRouter"
    335 +        elif "GROQ" in var_name:
    336 +            provider = "Groq"
    337 +        results.append(
    338 +            {
    339 +                "id": var_name.lower(),
    340 +                "provider": provider,
    341 +                "source_file": str(script_path),
    342 +                "models": model_ids,
    343 +            }
    344 +        )
    345 +    return results
    346 +
    347 +
    348 +def key_sensitivity(key_name: str) -> str:
    349 +    upper = key_name.upper()
    350 +    if any(token in upper for token in ("SECRET", "TOKEN", "API_KEY", "ACCESS_KEY")):
    351 +        return "secret"
    352 +    if upper.endswith("_ID") or "_ID_" in upper:
    353 +        return "identifier"
    354 +    if "URL" in upper or "ENDPOINT" in upper:
    355 +        return "endpoint"
    356 +    if upper.endswith("_PATH") or "CREDENTIALS" in upper:
    357 +        return "path_or_file_reference"
    358 +    if upper.startswith("NODE_ENV") or upper.endswith("ENVIRONMENT") or upper in {"PORT"}:
    359 +        return "runtime_config"
    360 +    return "config"
    361 +
    362 +
    363 +def key_description(key_name: str) -> str:
    364 +    if key_name in KEY_DESCRIPTIONS:
    365 +        return KEY_DESCRIPTIONS[key_name]
    366 +    upper = key_name.upper()
    367 +    if upper.endswith("_API_KEY"):
    368 +        return "API credential key used for provider authentication."
    369 +    if upper.endswith("_TOKEN"):
    370 +        return "Access token used for API or platform authorization."
    371 +    if upper.endswith("_SECRET"):
    372 +        return "Secret credential value that should be stored securely."
    373 +    if upper.endswith("_ID"):
    374 +        return "Identifier used to target a project, account, service, or resource."
    375 +    if "ENVIRONMENT" in upper:
    376 +        return "Runtime mode selector used to switch behavior across environments."
    377 +    if "CREDENTIALS" in upper:
    378 +        return "Credential reference or blob required for authenticated access."
    379 +    return "Configuration variable used in access workflows."
    380 +
    381 +
    382 +def build_dictionary(
    383 +    env_keys: Iterable[str],
    384 +    providers: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    385 +    key_sources: Dict[str, List[str]],
    386 +) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    387 +    provider_refs_by_key: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, str]]] = {}
    388 +    for provider in providers:
    389 +        provider_name = provider.get("provider", "")
    390 +        product_name = provider.get("product", "")
    391 +        for key in provider.get("env_vars", []):
    392 +            provider_refs_by_key.setdefault(key, []).append((provider_name, product_name))
    393 +
    394 +    dictionary: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    395 +    for key in sorted(set(env_keys)):
    396 +        refs = provider_refs_by_key.get(key, [])
    397 +        sectors = sorted({sector_for_provider({"provider": p, "product": prod, "env_vars": [key]}) for p, prod in refs})
    398 +        dictionary[key] = {
    399 +            "key": key,
    400 +            "sensitivity": key_sensitivity(key),
    401 +            "description": key_description(key),
    402 +            "provider_refs": [{"provider": p, "product": prod} for p, prod in refs],
    403 +            "sectors": sectors,
    404 +            "observed_in": sorted(set(key_sources.get(key, []))),
    405 +        }
    406 +    return dictionary
    407 +
    408 +
    409 +def provider_public_view(provider: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    410 +    clean = dict(provider)
    411 +    clean["sector"] = sector_for_provider(provider)
    412 +    return clean
    413 +
    414 +
    415 +def build_sector_index(providers: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    416 +    sector_map: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    417 +    for provider in providers:
    418 +        sector = sector_for_provider(provider)
    419 +        item = sector_map.setdefault(
    420 +            sector,
    421 +            {
    422 +                "sector": sector,
    423 +                "description": SECTOR_DESCRIPTIONS.get(sector, "No description set."),
    424 +                "providers": [],
    425 +                "env_keys": set(),
    426 +            },
    427 +        )
    428 +        item["providers"].append(provider["provider"])
    429 +        item["env_keys"].update(provider.get("env_vars", []))
    430 +
    431 +    normalized: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    432 +    for sector, item in sorted(sector_map.items()):
    433 +        normalized[sector] = {
    434 +            "sector": sector,
    435 +            "description": item["description"],
    436 +            "providers": sorted(set(item["providers"])),
    437 +            "env_keys": sorted(item["env_keys"]),
    438 +        }
    439 +    return normalized
    440 +
    441 +
    442 +def write_json(path: Path, payload: Any) -> None:
    443 +    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    444 +    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    445 +
    446 +
    447 +def write_models_yaml(path: Path, model_sets: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    448 +    lines: List[str] = []
    449 +    lines.append(f"generated_at: \"{now_iso()}\"")
    450 +    lines.append("model_sets:")
    451 +    for model_set in model_sets:
    452 +        lines.append(f"  - id: \"{model_set['id']}\"")
    453 +        lines.append(f"    provider: \"{model_set['provider']}\"")
    454 +        lines.append(f"    source_file: \"{model_set['source_file']}\"")
    455 +        lines.append("    models:")
    456 +        for model in model_set["models"]:
    457 +            lines.append(f"      - \"{model}\"")
    458 +    if not model_sets:
    459 +        lines.append("  []")
    460 +    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    461 +    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    462 +
    463 +
    464 +def build_bundle(package_root: Path, sources: List[SourceProject]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    465 +    provider_records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    466 +    all_env_keys: set[str] = set()
    467 +    key_sources: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    468 +    source_summaries: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    469 +
    470 +    for source in sources:
    471 +        path = source.path.expanduser()
    472 +        exists = path.exists()
    473 +        env_keys = extract_env_keys(path) if exists else []
    474 +        for key in env_keys:
    475 +            all_env_keys.add(key)
    476 +            key_sources.setdefault(key, []).append(source.label)
    477 +
    478 +        source_provider_records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    479 +        source_provider_records.extend(read_api_catalog_providers(path / "api-catalog.json", source.label))
    480 +        source_provider_records.extend(extract_polygon_provider(path, source.label))
    481 +        source_provider_records.extend(infer_additional_providers(env_keys, source.label))
    482 +        provider_records.extend(source_provider_records)
    483 +
    484 +        source_summaries.append(
    485 +            {
    486 +                "label": source.label,
    487 +                "path": str(path),
    488 +                "exists": exists,
    489 +                "env_key_count": len(env_keys),
    490 +                "provider_records_found": len(source_provider_records),
    491 +            }
    492 +        )
    493 +
    494 +    merged_providers = merge_providers(provider_records)
    495 +    public_providers = [provider_public_view(provider) for provider in merged_providers]
    496 +    for provider in public_providers:
    497 +        for key in provider.get("env_vars", []):
    498 +            all_env_keys.add(key)
    499 +            key_sources.setdefault(key, []).extend(provider.get("sources", []))
    500 +
    501 +    dictionary = build_dictionary(all_env_keys, public_providers, key_sources)
    502 +    sector_index = build_sector_index(public_providers)
    503 +
    504 +    aws_providers = [p for p in public_providers if p["sector"] == "cloud_aws"]
    505 +    gcloud_providers = [p for p in public_providers if p["sector"] == "cloud_gcp"]
    506 +
    507 +    model_sets: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    508 +    maplepay_script = next((source.path / "scripts" / "test_all_apis.py" for source in sources if source.label == "maplepay"), None)
    509 +    if maplepay_script:
    510 +        model_sets.extend(extract_models_from_file(maplepay_script))
    511 +
    512 +    write_json(
    513 +        package_root / "resources" / "aws.json",
    514 +        {
    515 +            "generated_at": now_iso(),
    516 +            "resource_group": "aws",
    517 +            "providers": aws_providers,
    518 +        },
    519 +    )
    520 +    write_json(
    521 +        package_root / "resources" / "gcloud.json",
    522 +        {
    523 +            "generated_at": now_iso(),
    524 +            "resource_group": "gcloud",
    525 +            "providers": gcloud_providers,
    526 +        },
    527 +    )
    528 +    write_json(
    529 +        package_root / "resources" / "api.json",
    530 +        {
    531 +            "generated_at": now_iso(),
    532 +            "resource_group": "api",
    533 +            "providers": public_providers,
    534 +        },
    535 +    )
    536 +    write_json(package_root / "data" / "access-dictionary.json", dictionary)
    537 +
    538 +    all_in_one = {
    539 +        "generated_at": now_iso(),
    540 +        "package": "access",
    541 +        "version": 1,
    542 +        "source_projects": source_summaries,
    543 +        "summary": {
    544 +            "provider_count": len(public_providers),
    545 +            "env_key_count": len(dictionary),
    546 +            "sector_count": len(sector_index),
    547 +            "model_set_count": len(model_sets),
    548 +        },
    549 +        "sectors": sector_index,
    550 +        "providers": public_providers,
    551 +        "env_dictionary": dictionary,
    552 +        "models": model_sets,
    553 +    }
    554 +    write_json(package_root / "data" / "access-all-in-one.json", all_in_one)
    555 +    write_models_yaml(package_root / "models" / "model-catalog.yaml", model_sets)
    556 +
    557 +    instance_id = f"{now_stamp()}__access-harvest"
    558 +    instance_root = package_root / "instances" / instance_id
    559 +    instance_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    560 +
    561 +    scouting_lines: List[str] = []
    562 +    scouting_lines.append("# Access Harvest Scouting Report")
    563 +    scouting_lines.append("")
    564 +    scouting_lines.append(f"- generated_at: {now_iso()}")
    565 +    scouting_lines.append(f"- provider_count: {len(public_providers)}")
    566 +    scouting_lines.append(f"- env_key_count: {len(dictionary)}")
    567 +    scouting_lines.append(f"- sector_count: {len(sector_index)}")
    568 +    scouting_lines.append("")
    569 +    scouting_lines.append("## Source Projects")
    570 +    for source_summary in source_summaries:
    571 +        scouting_lines.append(f"- {source_summary['label']}:")
    572 +        scouting_lines.append(f"  - path: `{source_summary['path']}`")
    573 +        scouting_lines.append(f"  - exists: {source_summary['exists']}")
    574 +        scouting_lines.append(f"  - env_key_count: {source_summary['env_key_count']}")
    575 +        scouting_lines.append(f"  - provider_records_found: {source_summary['provider_records_found']}")
    576 +    (instance_root / "scouting-report.md").write_text("\n".join(scouting_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    577 +
    578 +    write_json(
    579 +        instance_root / "manifest.json",
    580 +        {
    581 +            "generated_at": now_iso(),
    582 +            "instance_id": instance_id,
    583 +            "outputs": {
    584 +                "aws": str((package_root / "resources" / "aws.json").relative_to(package_root)),
    585 +                "gcloud": str((package_root / "resources" / "gcloud.json").relative_to(package_root)),
    586 +                "api": str((package_root / "resources" / "api.json").relative_to(package_root)),
    587 +                "all_in_one": str((package_root / "data" / "access-all-in-one.json").relative_to(package_root)),
    588 +                "dictionary": str((package_root / "data" / "access-dictionary.json").relative_to(package_root)),
    589 +                "models": str((package_root / "models" / "model-catalog.yaml").relative_to(package_root)),
    590 +            },
    591 +            "sources": source_summaries,
    592 +        },
    593 +    )
    594 +
    595 +    return {
    596 +        "instance_id": instance_id,
    597 +        "provider_count": len(public_providers),
    598 +        "env_key_count": len(dictionary),
    599 +        "sector_count": len(sector_index),
    600 +        "model_set_count": len(model_sets),
    601 +        "instance_root": str(instance_root),
    602 +    }
    603 +
    604 +

• Added appendix/toolkit/packages/access/scripts/generate_env_test_spec.py (+186 -0)
      1 +#!/usr/bin/env python3
      2 +"""Generate environment test spec from access all-in-one catalog."""
      3 +
      4 +from __future__ import annotations
      5 +
      6 +import argparse
      7 +import json
      8 +import re
      9 +from datetime import datetime
     10 +from pathlib import Path
     11 +from typing import Any, Dict, List, Set
     12 +
     13 +
     14 +def now_iso() -> str:
     15 +    return datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
     16 +
     17 +
     18 +def load_json(path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
     19 +    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
     20 +
     21 +
     22 +def read_env_keys(env_file: Path) -> Set[str]:
     23 +    keys: Set[str] = set()
     24 +    if not env_file.exists():
     25 +        return keys
     26 +    for line in env_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines():
     27 +        line = line.strip()
     28 +        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
     29 +            continue
     30 +        match = re.match(r"([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)=", line)
     31 +        if match:
     32 +            keys.add(match.group(1))
     33 +    return keys
     34 +
     35 +
     36 +def keys_for_profile(catalog: Dict[str, Any], profile: str) -> List[str]:
     37 +    profile = profile.lower().strip()
     38 +    providers = catalog.get("providers", [])
     39 +    if profile == "all":
     40 +        keys = set(catalog.get("env_dictionary", {}).keys())
     41 +        return sorted(keys)
     42 +
     43 +    keys: Set[str] = set()
     44 +    for provider in providers:
     45 +        sector = provider.get("sector", "")
     46 +        if profile.startswith("sector:"):
     47 +            wanted = profile.split(":", 1)[1]
     48 +            if sector == wanted:
     49 +                keys.update(provider.get("env_vars", []))
     50 +            continue
     51 +        if profile in {"aws", "gcloud", "api"}:
     52 +            if profile == "aws" and sector == "cloud_aws":
     53 +                keys.update(provider.get("env_vars", []))
     54 +            elif profile == "gcloud" and sector == "cloud_gcp":
     55 +                keys.update(provider.get("env_vars", []))
     56 +            elif profile == "api":
     57 +                keys.update(provider.get("env_vars", []))
     58 +            continue
     59 +        if sector == profile:
     60 +            keys.update(provider.get("env_vars", []))
     61 +
     62 +    return sorted(keys)
     63 +
     64 +
     65 +def yaml_quote(value: str) -> str:
     66 +    return '"' + value.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"') + '"'
     67 +
     68 +
     69 +def write_yaml(path: Path, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
     70 +    lines: List[str] = []
     71 +    meta = payload["meta"]
     72 +    summary = payload["summary"]
     73 +
     74 +    lines.append("meta:")
     75 +    lines.append(f"  generated_at: {yaml_quote(meta['generated_at'])}")
     76 +    lines.append(f"  profile: {yaml_quote(meta['profile'])}")
     77 +    lines.append(f"  catalog_file: {yaml_quote(meta['catalog_file'])}")
     78 +    lines.append(f"  env_file: {yaml_quote(meta['env_file'])}")
     79 +    lines.append("")
     80 +    lines.append("summary:")
     81 +    lines.append(f"  required_total: {summary['required_total']}")
     82 +    lines.append(f"  present_total: {summary['present_total']}")
     83 +    lines.append(f"  missing_total: {summary['missing_total']}")
     84 +    lines.append(f"  status: {yaml_quote(summary['status'])}")
     85 +    lines.append("")
     86 +    lines.append("checks:")
     87 +    for check in payload["checks"]:
     88 +        lines.append(f"  - id: {yaml_quote(check['id'])}")
     89 +        lines.append(f"    key: {yaml_quote(check['key'])}")
     90 +        lines.append(f"    present: {str(check['present']).lower()}")
     91 +        lines.append(f"    sensitivity: {yaml_quote(check['sensitivity'])}")
     92 +        lines.append("    sectors:")
     93 +        for sector in check["sectors"]:
     94 +            lines.append(f"      - {yaml_quote(sector)}")
     95 +        lines.append("    providers:")
     96 +        for provider in check["providers"]:
     97 +            lines.append(f"      - {yaml_quote(provider)}")
     98 +    lines.append("")
     99 +    lines.append("missing_keys:")
    100 +    for key in payload["missing_keys"]:
    101 +        lines.append(f"  - {yaml_quote(key)}")
    102 +
    103 +    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    104 +    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    105 +
    106 +
    107 +def build_spec(catalog: Dict[str, Any], profile: str, env_keys: Set[str], catalog_path: Path, env_file: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    108 +    dictionary = catalog.get("env_dictionary", {})
    109 +    selected_keys = keys_for_profile(catalog, profile)
    110 +    checks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    111 +
    112 +    for index, key in enumerate(selected_keys, 1):
    113 +        item = dictionary.get(key, {})
    114 +        providers = [ref.get("provider", "") for ref in item.get("provider_refs", []) if isinstance(ref, dict)]
    115 +        checks.append(
    116 +            {
    117 +                "id": f"ACCESS-ENV-{index:03d}",
    118 +                "key": key,
    119 +                "present": key in env_keys,
    120 +                "sensitivity": item.get("sensitivity", "config"),
    121 +                "sectors": item.get("sectors", []),
    122 +                "providers": sorted(set([p for p in providers if p])),
    123 +            }
    124 +        )
    125 +
    126 +    missing = [check["key"] for check in checks if not check["present"]]
    127 +    summary = {
    128 +        "required_total": len(checks),
    129 +        "present_total": len(checks) - len(missing),
    130 +        "missing_total": len(missing),
    131 +        "status": "PASS" if not missing else "FAIL",
    132 +    }
    133 +
    134 +    return {
    135 +        "meta": {
    136 +            "generated_at": now_iso(),
    137 +            "profile": profile,
    138 +            "catalog_file": str(catalog_path),
    139 +            "env_file": str(env_file),
    140 +        },
    141 +        "summary": summary,
    142 +        "checks": checks,
    143 +        "missing_keys": missing,
    144 +    }
    145 +
    146 +
    147 +def make_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    148 +    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Generate env test YAML from access catalog.")
    149 +    parser.add_argument(
    150 +        "--catalog",
    151 +        default="appendix/toolkit/packages/access/data/access-all-in-one.json",
    152 +        help="Path to access all-in-one JSON.",
    153 +    )
    154 +    parser.add_argument("--env-file", required=True, help="Path to .env file to validate.")
    155 +    parser.add_argument(
    156 +        "--profile",
    157 +        default="all",
    158 +        help="Profile: all | aws | gcloud | api | <sector> | sector:<sector_name>",
    159 +    )
    160 +    parser.add_argument(
    161 +        "--output",
    162 +        default="appendix/toolkit/packages/access/output/access-env-test.yaml",
    163 +        help="Output YAML test spec path.",
    164 +    )
    165 +    return parser
    166 +
    167 +
    168 +def main() -> int:
    169 +    args = make_parser().parse_args()
    170 +    catalog_path = Path(args.catalog).expanduser().resolve()
    171 +    env_path = Path(args.env_file).expanduser().resolve()
    172 +    output_path = Path(args.output).expanduser().resolve()
    173 +
    174 +    catalog = load_json(catalog_path)
    175 +    env_keys = read_env_keys(env_path)
    176 +    payload = build_spec(catalog, args.profile, env_keys, catalog_path, env_path)
    177 +    write_yaml(output_path, payload)
    178 +
    179 +    print(json.dumps(payload["summary"], indent=2))
    180 +    print(f"output={output_path}")
    181 +    return 0
    182 +
    183 +
    184 +if __name__ == "__main__":
    185 +    raise SystemExit(main())

• Added appendix/toolkit/packages/access/PACKAGE.md (+44 -0)
     1 +# Package: Access
     2 +
     3 +## Purpose
     4 +`access` is the credential and provider intelligence package for toolkit bootstraps.
     5 +
     6 +It consolidates multi-project access surfaces into:
     7 +1. split resource catalogs (`aws`, `gcloud`, `api`),
     8 +2. a single all-in-one JSON contract,
     9 +3. an env-key dictionary,
    10 +4. model catalog metadata,
    11 +5. rubric-driven environment test generation.
    12 +
    13 +## Responsibilities
    14 +1. Scout source projects and capture access-related provider metadata.
    15 +2. Normalize env-key names and provider mapping without storing secrets.
    16 +3. Classify providers into sectors and use-cases.
    17 +4. Generate deterministic env test specs for selected profiles/sectors.
    18 +5. Keep a reproducible harvest record under `instances/`.
    19 +
    20 +## Inputs
    21 +- Source project roots containing `.env`, `.env.example`, `api-catalog.json`, scripts, and docs.
    22 +- Optional profile selection when generating env tests (`all`, `aws`, `gcloud`, `api`, `sector:*`).
    23 +
    24 +## Outputs
    25 +- `resources/aws.json`
    26 +- `resources/gcloud.json`
    27 +- `resources/api.json`
    28 +- `data/access-all-in-one.json`
    29 +- `data/access-dictionary.json`
    30 +- `models/model-catalog.yaml`
    31 +- `instances/<timestamp>__access-harvest/{scouting-report.md,manifest.json}`
    32 +- `output/access-env-test.yaml` (generated on demand)
    33 +
    34 +## Command Contract (Recommended)
    35 +- Build resource/data bundle:
    36 +  - `python3 appendix/toolkit/packages/access/scripts/build_access_bundle.py`
    37 +- Generate env test spec:
    38 +  - `python3 appendix/toolkit/packages/access/scripts/generate_env_test_spec.py --env-file /path/to/.env --profile all`
    39 +
    40 +## Integration Notes
    41 +1. Run `access` before introducing a new package that depends on external providers.
    42 +2. Use `access-all-in-one.json` as the source of truth for ticket planning and env validation.
    43 +3. Keep setup-librarian incoming ticket history linked to the generated `instances/` manifest.
    44 +

• Added appendix/toolkit/packages/access/rubric/ACCESS_ENV_TEST_RUBRIC.md (+43 -0)
     1 +# Access Env Test Rubric
     2 +
     3 +Internal Use Only.
     4 +
     5 +This rubric governs how `access` generates environment test specs.
     6 +
     7 +## Stage 1: Explore
     8 +1. Confirm source projects exist and are readable.
     9 +2. Extract env key names from `.env` and `.env.example`.
    10 +3. Extract provider metadata from `api-catalog.json` (secret values must be ignored).
    11 +4. Capture extra provider signals from scripts/docs (for example Polygon market data).
    12 +
    13 +Pass gates:
    14 +- `resources/aws.json`, `resources/gcloud.json`, and `resources/api.json` are regenerated.
    15 +- No secret values appear in generated artifacts.
    16 +
    17 +## Stage 2: Decode
    18 +1. Map providers to sectors.
    19 +2. Build env-key dictionary with sensitivity, provider refs, and sector refs.
    20 +3. Build model catalog YAML from source script constants.
    21 +
    22 +Pass gates:
    23 +- `data/access-dictionary.json` includes every key found in resources.
    24 +- `models/model-catalog.yaml` includes named model sets when present.
    25 +
    26 +## Stage 3: Plan
    27 +1. Build unified contract in `data/access-all-in-one.json`.
    28 +2. Validate summary counts (providers, keys, sectors, model sets).
    29 +3. Produce one instance manifest in `instances/<timestamp>__access-harvest/`.
    30 +
    31 +Pass gates:
    32 +- All output paths exist.
    33 +- Instance manifest points to the latest generated resources.
    34 +
    35 +## Stage 4: Integrate
    36 +1. Generate env test YAML against a target `.env`.
    37 +2. Use profile-based checks (`all`, `aws`, `gcloud`, `api`, `sector:*`).
    38 +3. Report missing keys with deterministic IDs.
    39 +
    40 +Pass gates:
    41 +- Generated YAML includes `meta`, `summary`, `checks`, and `missing_keys`.
    42 +- Check IDs are stable format: `ACCESS-ENV-###`.

• Added appendix/toolkit/RESOURCE_EXAMPLE_CONTRACT.md (+45 -0)
     1 +# Resource Example Contract
     2 +
     3 +Internal Use Only.
     4 +
     5 +This contract defines how examples must be stored and validated in toolkit resources.
     6 +
     7 +## Rule 1: Example Location
     8 +Examples must live inside their owning resource.
     9 +
    10 +1. Package examples:
    11 +- `packages/<package_name>/examples/`
    12 +2. Workspace examples:
    13 +- `workspace/<authority_name>/examples/`
    14 +3. Setup examples:
    15 +- `setup/examples/`
    16 +- `setup/librarian/examples/`
    17 +
    18 +## Rule 2: Project-Named Paths
    19 +Every example must include the project name in its path.
    20 +
    21 +Examples:
    22 +1. `packages/domscan/examples/resuscitation_health_app/...`
    23 +2. `workspace/warden/examples/resuscitation_health_app/...`
    24 +3. `setup/examples/resuscitation_health_app/...`
    25 +
    26 +## Rule 3: Real Artifacts Only
    27 +Examples must come from real command output, not fabricated data.
    28 +
    29 +Allowed sources:
    30 +1. run outputs from `appendix/guidance/...`
    31 +2. run outputs from `workspace/<authority>/out/...`
    32 +3. command stdout captured from real command execution
    33 +
    34 +## Rule 4: Reproduction Metadata
    35 +Each resource example root must provide a `README.md` with:
    36 +1. project name
    37 +2. source run path
    38 +3. command(s) used to reproduce
    39 +4. list of included artifacts
    40 +
    41 +## Rule 5: Customization Compatibility
    42 +Custom projects follow the same convention:
    43 +1. run resource command(s) on target project
    44 +2. copy resulting artifacts into that resource's `examples/<project_name>/...`
    45 +3. update resource-local `README.md` index

• Added appendix/toolkit/setup/validate_real_examples.sh (+41 -0)
     1 +#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2 +set -euo pipefail
     3 +
     4 +ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
     5 +
     6 +required_paths=(
     7 +  "$ROOT_DIR/RESOURCE_EXAMPLE_CONTRACT.md"
     8 +  "$ROOT_DIR/packages/blueprint/examples/README.md"
     9 +  "$ROOT_DIR/packages/blueprint/examples/resuscitation_health_app/20260221-205714-warden/run_manifest.json"
    10 +  "$ROOT_DIR/packages/domscan/examples/README.md"
    11 +  "$ROOT_DIR/packages/domscan/examples/resuscitation_health_app/20260221-205725/run_manifest.json"
    12 +  "$ROOT_DIR/packages/checker/examples/README.md"
    13 +  "$ROOT_DIR/packages/checker/examples/resuscitation_health_app/checker-parity-output.txt"
    14 +  "$ROOT_DIR/packages/access/examples/README.md"
    15 +  "$ROOT_DIR/packages/access/output/maplepay-access-env-test.yaml"
    16 +  "$ROOT_DIR/workspace/warden/examples/README.md"
    17 +  "$ROOT_DIR/workspace/warden/examples/resuscitation_health_app/REQ-001-MASTER-SYSTEM.yaml"
    18 +  "$ROOT_DIR/workspace/headmaster/examples/README.md"
    19 +  "$ROOT_DIR/workspace/headmaster/examples/resuscitation_health_app/20260221-213322/operator-manifest.md"
    20 +  "$ROOT_DIR/setup/examples/README.md"
    21 +  "$ROOT_DIR/setup/examples/resuscitation_health_app/master-operator/20260221-213539/operator-manifest.md"
    22 +  "$ROOT_DIR/setup/librarian/examples/README.md"
    23 +  "$ROOT_DIR/setup/librarian/examples/resuscitation_health_app/TKL-003.yaml"
    24 +)
    25 +
    26 +missing=0
    27 +for p in "${required_paths[@]}"; do
    28 +  if [[ ! -e "$p" ]]; then
    29 +    echo "MISSING: $p"
    30 +    missing=$((missing + 1))
    31 +  fi
    32 +done
    33 +
    34 +if [[ "$missing" -gt 0 ]]; then
    35 +  echo
    36 +  echo "validate_real_examples: FAIL ($missing missing paths)"
    37 +  exit 1
    38 +fi
    39 +
    40 +echo "validate_real_examples: PASS"
    41 +echo "validated_paths: ${#required_paths[@]}"

• Yes, you can safely detach and move it.

  What I verified:

  - Core entrypoints are relocatable (appendix/toolkit/toolkit.sh, appendix/toolkit/setup/master_operator.sh) because they resolve paths from their own script location.
  - No symlinks inside appendix/toolkit.

  What to watch:

  - package.json scripts in this project point to appendix/toolkit/..., so those npm aliases will break after moving toolkit unless you update them.
  - Some example/run artifacts contain old absolute paths and resuscitation_health_app names in file content; this is historical metadata, not runtime logic.
  - appendix/toolkit/setup/validate_real_examples.sh is currently tied to the existing real example set.

  Safe usage after move:

  - Run directly from new toolkit location:
      - bash /new/path/toolkit/toolkit.sh plan --project-root /path/to/target --workspace warden --scan-package domscan
      - bash /new/path/toolkit/toolkit.sh apply --project-root /path/to/target --workspace warden --scan-package domscan

  If you want, I can now patch this project’s package.json to call toolkit from an external path/env variable so both local and detached modes work.
